Plan must: (1) Audit current reigh-worker->Wan2GP integration: every task type, memory-profile system (lowvram/medvram/highvram/profiled/etc.), model load/unload lifecycle, queue/dispatch shape, error paths, telemetry, worker-orchestrator coupling. (2) Audit VibeComfy capabilities: ready_templates, VibeWorkflow IR, validation, embedded local execution, RunPod execution path, gaps vs Wan2GP (especially memory profiles).         Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Migrate reigh-worker execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy

## Overview
Author a migration plan document at `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` covering the cutover of `reigh-worker`'s in-process generation backend from vendored `Wan2GP/wgp.py` (orchestrated via `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) to `vibecomfy` (Python toolkit around ComfyUI, with `VibeWorkflow` IR, `ready_templates/`, embedded + RunPod runtimes in `vibecomfy/runtime/`).

Key constraints discovered while scoping:
- Memory profiles are non-negotiable: today driven by `--wgp-profile {1..5}` in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:556-566` mapping to mmgp's `force_profile_no` / `default_profile`, plus per-task `override_profile` in `source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py:166,237,374`. VibeComfy currently has no equivalent abstraction; ComfyUI relies on its own VRAM management knobs (`--lowvram`, `--novram`, `--gpu-only`, `--cpu`) and `vibecomfy/runtime/server.py` does not expose them as a profile system.
- Task surface to preserve: per `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, ~25+ task types across VACE (`vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`), Flux, T2V/I2V (`t2v`, `t2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`), Hunyuan, LTX (`ltxv`, `ltx2`), Qwen image (`qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_hires`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`), Z-Image (`z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`), plus orchestrated handlers (`travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `join_clips_*`, `inpaint_frames`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`).
- Coupling beyond generation: orchestrator + WanOrchestrator handles dynamic model definition load (`load_missing_model_definition`), LoRA setup (`source/models/wgp/lora_setup.py`, `wgp_patches.py:389-445`), Uni3C ControlNet caching, RIFE temporal interpolation, Qwen prompt expander, Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotators, save-video helpers — all currently re-exported through `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` and `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`.
- Orchestrator coupling: `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:463` invokes `--wgp-profile 1`. Worker image install of Wan2GP is part of the runtime contract.
- VibeComfy ready templates already cover most target families: `ready_templates/image/{flux2_klein_*,qwen_image_2512,z_image}.py`, `ready_templates/video/{wan_*,ltx2_3_*,wanvideo_wrapper_*}.py` — but not 1:1 with the worker's task-type→model mapping in `task_types.TASK_TYPE_TO_MODEL`.

The deliverable is a planning markdown doc; no code changes in this phase. The executor will write to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Frame the document and write metadata header (`docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open with H1 `# reigh-worker → VibeComfy Migration Plan`, status banner ("Draft, 2026-05-05"), one-paragraph summary, and a clickable TOC.
2. State the goals (preserve external worker contract: queue payloads, output shapes, per-task SLOs, memory-profile guarantees) and explicit non-goals (no Supabase queue schema rework, no orchestrator algorithmic changes beyond image/runtime swap, no `reigh-app` UI/API changes).
3. Reference authoritative source paths: `reigh-worker/source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`, `reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/{run.py,session.py,server.py,client.py}`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py`.

### Step 2: Audit current reigh-worker → Wan2GP integration (Section 1)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document runtime entry path: `run_worker.py` → `source/runtime/worker/server.py` (CLI flags `--wgp-attention-mode`, `--wgp-compile`, `--wgp-profile`, `--wgp-vae-config`, `--wgp-boost`, `--wgp-transformer-quantization`, `--wgp-transformer-dtype-policy`, `--wgp-text-encoder-quantization`, `--wgp-vae-precision`, `--wgp-mixed-precision`, `--wgp-preload-policy`, `--wgp-preload`) → `import wgp as wgp_mod` (`server.py:551`) → `HeadlessTaskQueue` (`task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) → `WanOrchestrator` (`models/wgp/orchestrator.py`).
2. Enumerate memory-profile system: profile values 1–5 with display names from `server.py:605-609`, mmgp `force_profile_no`/`default_profile`, per-task `override_profile` in `wgp_params.py`, default-profile-3 today via `start_worker.bat:14`, `worker_startup.template.sh:463`, `scripts/live_test/main.py:27`, `scripts/live_test/smoke.py:27`.
3. Tabulate all task types (cols: task_type, default_model, is_direct_queue, is_wgp_output, allows_empty_prompt, forced_video_length, current handler module). Source: `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` and `task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py` import block.
4. Diagram queue/dispatch: `worker.py` poll → `db_task_to_generation_task` (`task_conversion.py`) → direct-queue (`HeadlessTaskQueue.submit_task`) or specialized handler (travel/join/inpaint/edit/visualize) which itself enqueues child WGP tasks. Capture lazy bootstrap pattern in `task_handlers/queue/wgp_init.py`.
5. Document model lifecycle: `load_model_impl`/`unload_model_impl`/`load_missing_model_definition` in `source/models/wgp/model_ops.py`, dynamic JSON model defs at `Wan2GP/profiles/*` and `Wan2GP/defaults/*`, runtime-mutable hooks in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/runtime_registry.py`, LoRA flow (`source/models/wgp/lora_setup.py`, `wgp_patches.py:389-445`).
6. Document vendor surface re-exported via `wgp_bridge` + `vendor_imports`: Qwen prompt expander, Canny/DepthV2/Flow/Pose annotators, `flow_viz`, `run_rife_temporal_interpolation`, `load_uni3c_controlnet`, Wan2GP `save_video`, shared LoRA utils, Qwen family handler, Qwen main module. Each row gets a "VibeComfy equivalent / gap" cell to be filled in Section 3.
7. Document error paths and telemetry: `source/models/wgp/error_extraction.py`, `core/log/core.py:59,112`, `task_handlers/queue/memory_cleanup.py`, `cleanup_memory_after_task`, `model_patch_session.py`, `status_wait.py`, memory-stats logging in `wgp/generators/output.py`.
8. Document orchestrator coupling: `worker_startup.template.sh:463` `--wgp-profile 1`; `pyproject.toml` pins `mmgp==3.7.6`; `--queue-workers` controls capacity; pod disk 50GB sized for one Wan2GP family at a time.

### Step 3: Audit VibeComfy capabilities (Section 2)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document `VibeWorkflow` IR (`vibecomfy/vibecomfy/workflow.py`): `add_node`/`connect`/`disconnect`/`replace_edge`/`finalize_metadata`/`compile("api")` per `vibecomfy/docs/authoring.md`. Rule: changes-handles → template, decorates-handles → patch.
2. Document ready_templates organization (`ready_templates/{image,video,edit,audio}/`) and current 46-template corpus per `ready_templates/README.md`. Map relevant templates to each reigh-worker task type (e.g., `image/z_image.py` ↔ `z_image_turbo`, `image/qwen_image_2512.py` ↔ `qwen_image_2512`, `video/wan_t2v.py` ↔ `t2v`/`t2v_22`, `video/wan_i2v.py` ↔ `i2v`/`i2v_22`, `video/ltx2_3_t2v.py` ↔ `ltx2`, `video/wanvideo_wrapper_*` for VACE / FLF2V / fun-control / camera).
3. Document runtime: `run_embedded`/`run_embedded_sync` (in-process via `EmbeddedSession`, `runtime/session.py`) vs `run`/`run_sync` (external Comfy server, `runtime/{client.py,server.py}`). `EmbeddedSession` boots managed local ComfyUI per `runtime/server.py:comfy_server`. Capture schema-gate warning emitted by `_build_schema_provider`.
4. Document validation: `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/schema/{validate.py,cache.py,format.py,provider.py}` plus `vibecomfy doctor`.
5. Document RunPod path: VibeComfy delegates pod lifecycle to `runpod-lifecycle` package via `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` — list, status, terminate, gpu-types. Remote execution still goes through `run(server_url=...)` to a remote Comfy server. For reigh-worker, embedded runtime is the analogue of vendored `wgp.py`.
6. Document explicit gaps: confirm by grep that VibeComfy has no `profile`/`override_profile`/`force_profile_no` system, no LoRA-key sanitizer equivalent, no Uni3C ControlNet cache, no RIFE temporal interpolation helper, no Qwen prompt expander wrapper, no Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotator re-exports, no Wan2GP `save_video` callable. Each becomes a row in the Section 3 gap table.

### Step 4: Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (Section 3)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Author "Parity Gap Matrix" table (cols: capability, current Wan2GP location, VibeComfy status, required work, owner-repo).
2. Specify memory-profile abstraction (P0): new `vibecomfy.runtime.profile` module with `MemoryProfile` enum (MAX_PERFORMANCE=1, HIGH_RAM=2, BALANCED=3, CONSERVATIVE=4, MINIMUM=5), `ProfileSpec` mapping to ComfyUI launch flags + per-template policy hooks (loader class swaps, GGUF substitution, tile sizes); `EmbeddedSession`/`SessionConfig` accept `profile`; per-call `override_profile` on `run_embedded`. Acceptance: per-profile parity smoke tests for representative model per family (Wan T2V, Wan I2V, LTX2, Qwen, Z-Image, Flux), measuring peak VRAM and wall-clock vs Wan2GP baseline.
3. Specify task-type → ready_template registry: new `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` maps each `TASK_TYPE_TO_MODEL` entry to a VibeComfy template id and a parameter adapter; explicit "no template yet" rows block cutover until added.
4. Specify vendor-utility shims: RIFE keep vendored under `reigh-worker/source/media/`; Uni3C as VibeComfy patch on Wan2.2 templates or load directly inside handler; Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow pre-process before workflow build (preferred); Qwen prompt expander pre-process; LoRA-key sanitizer becomes a VibeComfy patch over `LoraLoader` nodes.
5. Specify dynamic-model-definition decision: recommend build-time freeze (ship templates upfront), call out as Open Question Q1.
6. Specify observability shim: translate `RunResult` (`run_id`, `prompt_id`, `outputs`, `metadata_path`, `log_path`) into existing telemetry (heartbeat logs, `system_logs` table, status_display strings).

### Step 5: Sprint-by-sprint migration plan (Section 4)
**Scope:** Large
1. Sprint table (≤2 wk each), each listing: goals, shippable artifacts, exit criteria, owner, risk, rollback move.
   - **Sprint 0 (1 wk)** Discovery freeze + benchmark harness via `scripts/run_worker_matrix.py` + `scripts/live_test/`. Output: `reigh-worker/docs/migration-baselines.md`.
   - **Sprint 1 (2 wk)** VibeComfy memory-profile MVP (Section 3 §2). Exit: profiles 1–5 round-trip on `image/z_image` and `video/wan_t2v`.
   - **Sprint 2 (2 wk)** Adapter shim in reigh-worker: new `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` + `source/models/comfy/comfy_backend.py` (parallel to `WanOrchestrator`), feature flag `REIGH_BACKEND={wgp|comfy}`. Wire safest tasks first: `z_image_turbo`, `qwen_image_2512`, `t2v`. Exit: feature-flagged Comfy path runs end-to-end in dev.
   - **Sprint 3 (2 wk)** Dual-execution + comparison harness `scripts/dual_run_compare.py` (image hash, video frame-pHash, frame count, dimensions, audio length, latency, VRAM, OOM count). Nightly across Sprint 0 matrix. Exit: green report ≥80% of task types.
   - **Sprint 4 (2 wk)** Wan-family parity: `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`. Exit: dual-run parity green for Wan family.
   - **Sprint 5 (2 wk)** LTX, Hunyuan, Flux, Qwen parity + move pre-processing pipeline (Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow). Exit: dual-run parity green for remaining direct-queue tasks.
   - **Sprint 6 (2 wk)** Orchestrated handlers: `travel_segment`/`individual_travel_segment`, `join_clips_*`, `inpaint_frames`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `magic_edit`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate`. Exit: full task-type catalog runnable through Comfy backend.
   - **Sprint 7 (2 wk)** Canary in production by `task_type` cohort using server-side selector (DB column or env). Hold each cohort 48h before promoting next.
   - **Sprint 8 (1 wk)** Wan2GP removal: delete `Wan2GP/`, `source/runtime/wgp_*`, `source/models/wgp/`, WGP-override block in `server.py:544-595`, `--wgp-*` CLI flags (alias `--vibecomfy-profile`); update `worker_startup.template.sh:463`; drop `mmgp` from `pyproject.toml`; revisit `disk_size_gb`.
2. Critical path callout: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3, then 4/5 in parallel, then 6 → 7 → 8.

### Step 6: Per-task-type cutover order with rationale (Section 5)
**Scope:** Small
1. Cohort table:
   - **A** lowest risk, image-only deterministic: `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_2512`, `flux`, `wan_2_2_t2i`.
   - **B** medium, image edits: `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`.
   - **C** medium-high, video gen: `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`.
   - **D** high, VACE + Hunyuan: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `hunyuan`.
   - **E** highest, orchestrated: `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `rife_interpolate`, `extract_frame`.
2. Canary mechanism: server-side `backend_for_task_type` map read at task claim time, with explicit "legacy" fallback while migrating each cohort.

### Step 7: Rollback plan, telemetry/observability, final removal (Sections 6–8)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Rollback: `REIGH_BACKEND` flag at worker process level via `--backend wgp|comfy` plus per-task-type override; both stacks remain in worker image until Sprint 8; orchestrator passes flag through `worker_startup.template.sh`. Trigger conditions: latency > p95 baseline by N%, new error-class spike, output-divergence rate > threshold. Document signals (`heartbeat_guardian` error classes, `system_logs` filters, dual-run delta thresholds).
2. Telemetry: add `backend` and `template_id` log labels; emit `vibecomfy.run_id` and `comfy.prompt_id` alongside task id; mirror VRAM logging from `wgp/generators/output.py:log_memory_stats` to Comfy path; capture `RunResult.log_path` into `source/core/log/debug_card.py`.
3. Final Wan2GP removal checklist: every file/dir/commit move (Sprint 8) including orchestrator side (Dockerfile/startup script, `pyproject.toml`/`uv.lock`/`requirements.txt`, `tests/test_wgp_patch_lifecycle.py` mmgp shims).

### Step 8: Open questions, assumptions, risks/mitigations (Section 9)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open questions:
   - Q1 Static vs dynamic VibeComfy template authoring (mirror of `load_missing_model_definition`)?
   - Q2 Does ComfyUI's per-process memory model accept a 5-tier profile mapping cleanly, or do some profiles require process restarts?
   - Q3 Output bit-exact reproduction is unrealistic — what divergence threshold defines "pass" in dual-run harness?
   - Q4 Pre-process annotators host: `reigh-worker` or VibeComfy plugins?
   - Q5 RunPod startup-time differences (image size, ComfyUI cold-boot)?
   - Q6 LoRA-key sanitizer (`wgp_patches.py:389-445`) — mmgp-specific or does ComfyUI break too?
2. Assumptions: queue contracts and Supabase schema unchanged; orchestrator continues single-GPU pods; `reigh-app` unaffected; `vibecomfy` repo is canonical home for new templates; `ready_templates/` is pre-vetted.
3. Risks + mitigations: missing profile system (Sprint 1 P0); embedded ComfyUI cold-boot dominating short-job latency (long-lived `EmbeddedSession`); template catalog drift (template-routing tests); LoRA divergence (golden-output corpus); pod disk too small during dual-run (Sprint 0 baselining + bump `disk_size_gb`).

### Step 9: Self-review checklist before handoff
**Scope:** Small
1. Verify exactly one H1, TOC IDs resolve, every referenced file path exists, every task type from `TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` appears in either audit table (Step 2) or cutover cohort table (Step 6), parity-gap matrix lists memory-profile system as P0.
2. Confirm no unsourced claims; each capability gap cites either a `vibecomfy/...` path or a documented absence (negative grep).

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1 → 2 → 3 in order (frame, then audit each side independently).
2. Step 4 depends on the gap rows surfaced by 2+3.
3. Steps 5, 6, 7 build on 4 and may be drafted in parallel.
4. Step 8 collected throughout, finalized last.
5. Step 9 final pass before handing back to executor.

## Validation Order
1. Executor writes `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` per this plan.
2. Reviewer verifies (a) file exists at exactly `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`, (b) every Step's required table/section is present with named columns, (c) each task type from `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` is referenced ≥1 time, (d) memory-profile gap is identified as P0, (e) sprints ≤2 weeks each and sequenced for incremental risk retirement, (f) rollback mechanism is concrete (named env flag + per-task override), (g) all source-code references resolve.

    "Queue contracts (Supabase task table schema, claim/status update flow, output upload paths) remain unchanged across the migration \u2014 only the in-process generation backend swaps.",
    "`reigh-worker-orchestrator` continues to provision single-GPU RunPod pods using its current spawner; the migration changes only the worker startup command and image, not the orchestration algorithm.",
    "`reigh-app` (frontend + edge functions) is unaffected; existing per-task UI controls map onto the new backend without API changes.",
    "VibeComfy at `./vibecomfy` is the canonical home for new templates and patches; reigh-worker depends on it as a Python package and does not fork its templates.",
    "The 5-tier memory-profile semantics (Max Performance / High RAM / Balanced / Conservative / Minimum) are preserved as the public contract \u2014 display strings in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:605-609` continue to surface in the worker UI/logs even after the underlying mechanism changes.",
    "Dual-running both backends temporarily increases pod disk requirements; we will revisit `disk_size_gb` (currently 50GB and already tight per project memory) during Sprint 0 baselining.",
    "Bit-exact output reproduction is not required; perceptual/structural equivalence within an agreed threshold is acceptable for cutover.",
    "Pre-processing helpers (Canny, DepthV2, Pose, Flow, RIFE, Uni3C, Qwen prompt expander) can be invoked from reigh-worker before the VibeComfy generation call rather than embedded inside ComfyUI workflows, unless a specific case requires graph-internal placement.",
    "Plan-phase output is the structured markdown below; the executor phase will be the only writer to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`."
  ],
  "structure_warnings": []
}

    "iteration": 1,
    "idea": "Produce a migration plan document for moving reigh-worker's execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy. Repos live side-by-side at ./reigh-worker (current Wan2GP consumer) and ./vibecomfy (target). Plan must: (1) Audit current reigh-worker->Wan2GP integration: every task type, memory-profile system (lowvram/medvram/highvram/profiled/etc.), model load/unload lifecycle, queue/dispatch shape, error paths, telemetry, worker-orchestrator coupling. (2) Audit VibeComfy capabilities: ready_templates, VibeWorkflow IR, validation, embedded local execution, RunPod execution path, gaps vs Wan2GP (especially memory profiles). (3) Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (memory-profile abstraction modeled on Wan2GP's, equivalents for every task type, model-management lifecycle). (4) Sprint-by-sprint migration plan, each sprint <=2 weeks, sequenced for incremental risk retirement (shadow/dual-run/canary before full cutover), with concrete shippable outcomes. Cover: VibeComfy parity work, adapter/shim in reigh-worker, dual-execution+comparison harness, per-task-type cutover order with rationale, rollback plan, telemetry/observability changes, final Wan2GP removal. (5) Open questions, assumptions, decision points, risks, mitigations. Context: reigh-worker uses Wan2GP today as in-process execution backend across multiple task types (image gen, video gen, edits, etc.). Memory profiles are non-negotiable \u2014 full parity required before cutover. VibeComfy is a Python toolkit around ComfyUI workflows with VibeWorkflow IR, ready_templates, embedded-local + RunPod execution. Migration should preserve reigh-worker's external behavior (queue contracts, output shapes, latency SLOs). Orchestrator (./reigh-worker-orchestrator) provisions GPU workers; coordinate worker-image/runtime changes with that. Output: docs/migration-vibecomfy.md.",

        {
  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-145",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },

    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

        "id": "DEBT-014",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-110",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        Robustness level:

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Migrate reigh-worker execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy

## Overview
Author a migration plan document at `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` covering the cutover of `reigh-worker`'s in-process generation backend from vendored `Wan2GP/wgp.py` (orchestrated via `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) to `vibecomfy` (Python toolkit around ComfyUI, with `VibeWorkflow` IR, `ready_templates/`, embedded + RunPod runtimes in `vibecomfy/runtime/`).

Key constraints discovered while scoping (verified against the working tree):
- **Memory profiles** are non-negotiable. Today: `--wgp-profile {1..5}` in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:556-566` mapping to mmgp's `force_profile_no` / `default_profile`, plus per-task `override_profile` in `source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py:166,237,374`. VibeComfy already exposes lower-level memory controls in `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:46-49` (`SessionConfig.vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`) and translates them into both server query values (`session.py:625-642`) and embedded launch argv (`session.py:660-678`). The 1–5 tier abstraction is missing — it must layer **on top of** the existing `SessionConfig` surface, not replace it.
- **Task surface to preserve** comes from the union of three sources, not just `TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`:
  - `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` (direct-queue catalog: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `flux`, `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `hunyuan`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`, `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `inpaint_frames`, `generate_video`, `join_clips_segment`).
  - `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:1442-1511` dispatch handlers (orchestrated/specialized): `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
  - `source/core/log/display_names.py:8-39` display aliases (note: `rife_interpolate` is the friendly alias; the **runtime task type is `rife_interpolate_images`** — the plan uses the runtime name everywhere and notes the alias).
- **Existing ComfyUI integration** must be audited, not duplicated. `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` and `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` already provide a lazy-launched ComfyUI server, an HTTP client, and the `comfy` task-type dispatch. The migration must decide reuse/extend/retire for these before proposing parallel files.
- **Coupling beyond generation**: `WanOrchestrator` handles dynamic model definition load (`load_missing_model_definition`), LoRA setup (`source/models/wgp/lora_setup.py`, `wgp_patches.py:389-445`), Uni3C ControlNet caching, RIFE temporal interpolation, Qwen prompt expander, Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotators, save-video helpers — all re-exported through `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` and `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`.
- **Orchestrator coupling**: `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:463` invokes `--wgp-profile 1`; Wan2GP install is part of the worker image contract.
- **VibeComfy ready templates** at `vibecomfy/ready_templates/{image,video,edit,audio}/` count **50 `.py` files** by live `find` count (the README.md figure of 46 is stale). Coverage gaps confirmed by directory inspection: **no Hunyuan template** exists in `ready_templates/video/` (only an `EmptyHunyuanLatentVideo` node reference inside Wan templates). Edit corpus is Flux/Qwen-edit only; audio corpus is ACE-Step + Qwen3 TTS.

The deliverable is a planning markdown doc; no code changes in this phase. The executor will write to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Frame the document and write metadata header (`docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open with H1 `# reigh-worker → VibeComfy Migration Plan`, status banner ("Draft, 2026-05-05"), one-paragraph summary, and a clickable TOC.
2. State goals (preserve external worker contract: queue payloads, output shapes, per-task SLOs, memory-profile guarantees) and explicit non-goals (no Supabase queue schema rework, no orchestrator algorithmic changes beyond image/runtime swap, no `reigh-app` UI/API changes).
3. Reference authoritative source paths up front: `reigh-worker/source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`, `reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}`, `reigh-worker/source/core/log/display_names.py`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/{run.py,session.py,server.py,client.py}`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py`, `vibecomfy/docs/runtime_surface.md`.

### Step 2: Audit current reigh-worker → Wan2GP integration (Section 1)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document runtime entry path: `run_worker.py` → `source/runtime/worker/server.py` (CLI flags `--wgp-attention-mode`, `--wgp-compile`, `--wgp-profile`, `--wgp-vae-config`, `--wgp-boost`, `--wgp-transformer-quantization`, `--wgp-transformer-dtype-policy`, `--wgp-text-encoder-quantization`, `--wgp-vae-precision`, `--wgp-mixed-precision`, `--wgp-preload-policy`, `--wgp-preload`) → `import wgp as wgp_mod` (`server.py:551`) → `HeadlessTaskQueue` (`task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) → `WanOrchestrator` (`models/wgp/orchestrator.py`).
2. Enumerate memory-profile system: profile values 1–5 with display names from `server.py:605-609` ("Max Performance", "High RAM", "Balanced", "Conservative", "Minimum"); mmgp `force_profile_no`/`default_profile` set at `server.py:556-558`; per-task `override_profile` in `source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py:166,237,374`; default-profile-3 today via `start_worker.bat:14`, `worker_startup.template.sh:463`, `scripts/live_test/main.py:27`, `scripts/live_test/smoke.py:27`.
3. Tabulate the **full task surface** in one table (cols: task_type, source-of-truth file, default_model, dispatch_path, current handler module, output shape, notes). Build the union of:
   - All entries in `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`.
   - All dispatch keys in `task_registry.py:1442-1511`: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
   - Friendly aliases from `source/core/log/display_names.py:8-39` (e.g., `rife_interpolate` → `rife_interpolate_images`).
   Include an explicit "alias for" column so the misnamed `rife_interpolate` cannot leak forward; the runtime name is `rife_interpolate_images`.
4. Diagram queue/dispatch: `worker.py` poll → `db_task_to_generation_task(params, task_id, task_type, context["wan2gp_path"], context["debug_mode"])` (`task_conversion.py`) → either `TaskRegistry._handle_direct_queue_task` (direct queue, top-level catalog tasks → `HeadlessTaskQueue.submit_task`) **or** specialized handler (orchestrated; the handler itself enqueues child WGP tasks via `context["task_queue"]`). Capture the lazy bootstrap pattern in `task_handlers/queue/wgp_init.py` (`OrchestratorBootstrapState`, retry/backoff). Call out explicitly that **two adapter seams** exist: (a) the direct-queue conversion path, and (b) the nested handler-created child-task path — the migration adapter must cover both.
5. Document model lifecycle: `load_model_impl`/`unload_model_impl`/`load_missing_model_definition` in `source/models/wgp/model_ops.py`, dynamic JSON model defs at `Wan2GP/profiles/*` and `Wan2GP/defaults/*`, runtime-mutable hooks in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/runtime_registry.py`, LoRA flow (`source/models/wgp/lora_setup.py`, `wgp_patches.py:389-445`).
6. Document vendor surface re-exported via `wgp_bridge` + `vendor_imports`: Qwen prompt expander, Canny/DepthV2/Flow/Pose annotators, `flow_viz`, `run_rife_temporal_interpolation`, `load_uni3c_controlnet`, Wan2GP `save_video`, shared LoRA utils, Qwen family handler, Qwen main module. Each row gets a "VibeComfy equivalent / gap" cell to be filled in Section 3.
7. Document the **existing ComfyUI integration**: `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` (lazy-launches a local ComfyUI subprocess, processes a `comfy` task type with raw workflow JSON), `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` (`ComfyUIManager`, `ComfyUIClient`, `COMFY_PATH`, `COMFY_PORT`), the `comfy` dispatch branch at `task_registry.py:1507-1510`, and tests that import these modules. State the migration's intent up front (decided in Section 3, §7): **retire** this handler's bespoke launcher in favor of `vibecomfy.runtime.session.EmbeddedSession`, and either **fold** the `comfy` task type into the generic VibeComfy backend (preferred) or keep it as a thin pass-through that submits raw API JSON via `vibecomfy.runtime.client.ComfyClient`.
8. Document error paths and telemetry: `source/models/wgp/error_extraction.py`, `core/log/core.py:59,112` (mmgp version warnings), `task_handlers/queue/memory_cleanup.py`, `cleanup_memory_after_task`, `model_patch_session.py`, `status_wait.py`, memory-stats logging in `wgp/generators/output.py`.
9. Document orchestrator coupling: `worker_startup.template.sh:463` `--wgp-profile 1`; `pyproject.toml` pins `mmgp==3.7.6`; `--queue-workers` controls capacity; pod disk 50 GB (per project memory, already tight) sized for one Wan2GP family at a time.

### Step 3: Audit VibeComfy capabilities (Section 2)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document `VibeWorkflow` IR (`vibecomfy/vibecomfy/workflow.py`): `add_node`/`connect`/`disconnect`/`replace_edge`/`finalize_metadata`/`compile("api")` per `vibecomfy/docs/authoring.md`. Rule: changes-handles → template, decorates-handles → patch.
2. Document ready_templates organization (`ready_templates/{image,video,edit,audio}/`). Use the **live `find` count of 50 `.py` files** (verified) and note the README's "46" figure is stale. Map known templates to each reigh-worker direct-queue task type (e.g., `image/z_image.py` ↔ `z_image_turbo`, `image/qwen_image_2512.py` ↔ `qwen_image_2512`, `video/wan_t2v.py` ↔ `t2v`/`t2v_22`, `video/wan_i2v.py` ↔ `i2v`/`i2v_22`, `video/ltx2_3_t2v.py` ↔ `ltx2`, `video/wanvideo_wrapper_*` for VACE / FLF2V / fun-control / camera). **Explicitly mark** task types with no template today as missing-template gaps to be addressed in Section 3 §3:
   - `hunyuan` — no `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` (verified by listing).
   - Any other catalog/dispatch task without a 1:1 template hit (executor enumerates the full table during write-up).
3. Document the runtime: `run_embedded`/`run_embedded_sync` (`runtime/run.py`) over `EmbeddedSession` (`runtime/session.py`); `run`/`run_sync` against an external Comfy server (`runtime/{client.py,server.py}`). Capture `_build_schema_provider` schema-gate warning behavior.
4. Document **existing memory controls** on `SessionConfig` (`vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:36-52`): `vram_policy: str = "auto"` (`high|low|normal|auto`), `reserve_vram_gb: float | None`, `cache_policy: str = "smart"` (`smart|classic|none|lru:N`), `disable_smart_memory: bool`. Translation paths verified at `session.py:625-642` (server query values: `highvram`/`lowvram`/`normalvram`, `reserve_vram`, `cache_classic`/`cache_none`/`cache_lru`, `disable_smart_memory`) and `session.py:660-678` (embedded launch argv: `--highvram`/`--lowvram`/`--normalvram`, `--reserve-vram`, `--disable-smart-memory`, `--cache-classic`/`--cache-none`/`--cache-lru N`). The migration's profile abstraction must layer on these knobs.
5. Document validation: `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/schema/{validate.py,cache.py,format.py,provider.py}` plus `vibecomfy doctor`.
6. Document RunPod path: VibeComfy delegates pod lifecycle to `runpod-lifecycle` via `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` (list/status/terminate/gpu-types). Remote execution still goes through `run(server_url=...)`. For reigh-worker, `EmbeddedSession` is the analogue of vendored `wgp.py`.
7. Document explicit gaps confirmed by negative grep / directory scan: no 1–5 profile tier abstraction (only the lower-level knobs in §4); no LoRA-key sanitizer equivalent (cf. `wgp_patches.py:389-445`); no Uni3C ControlNet cache; no RIFE temporal interpolation helper; no Qwen prompt expander wrapper; no Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotator re-exports; no Wan2GP `save_video` callable; **no Hunyuan ready_template**. Each becomes a row in the Section 3 gap table.

### Step 4: Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (Section 3)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Author "Parity Gap Matrix" table (cols: capability, current Wan2GP location, VibeComfy current state, required work, P-priority, owner-repo, target sprint).
2. Specify **memory-profile abstraction (P0)** as a thin layer over the existing `SessionConfig` knobs (not greenfield):
   - New `vibecomfy.runtime.profile` module exposing `MemoryProfile` enum (`MAX_PERFORMANCE=1`, `HIGH_RAM=2`, `BALANCED=3`, `CONSERVATIVE=4`, `MINIMUM=5`) and a `MemoryProfile.to_session_overrides()` helper that returns a partial dict mapping into `SessionConfig` fields (`vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`) plus optional per-template policy hooks (loader class swaps, GGUF substitution, tile sizes — same shape as the existing "LTX low-VRAM" patch policy in `vibecomfy/docs/authoring.md`).
   - Concrete starting mapping (to be tuned in Sprint 1): MAX_PERFORMANCE → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; HIGH_RAM → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="lru:32"`; BALANCED → `vram_policy="normal"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; CONSERVATIVE → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="classic"`, `reserve_vram_gb=2.0`; MINIMUM → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="none"`, `disable_smart_memory=True`, `reserve_vram_gb=4.0`.
   - `EmbeddedSession`/`SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata` accept `profile: MemoryProfile` (overlays defaults but lets explicit `vram_policy`/`cache_policy` win); per-call `override_profile` arg on `run_embedded` matches `wgp_params.py`'s semantics (Open Question Q2 covers whether overrides require process restart).
   - Acceptance: per-profile parity smoke tests for representative model per family (Wan T2V, Wan I2V, LTX2, Qwen, Z-Image, Flux), measuring peak VRAM and wall-clock vs Wan2GP baseline.
3. Specify **task-type → VibeComfy template registry**. Mapping lives in `reigh-worker` (not VibeComfy) at a new module location to be decided in §7 below. The registry must cover the **union task surface** assembled in Step 2 §3 — direct-queue catalog plus orchestrated-handler internal sub-tasks (the WGP child tasks they enqueue). Explicit "no template yet" rows block cutover; the executor table will list each missing template with target sprint and template author.
4. Specify **missing-template gaps with explicit P-priority and go/no-go**:
   - **Hunyuan (P0 for Cohort D / "VACE + Hunyuan" sprint)** — no `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` exists (verified). Cohort D cutover **must not start** until a Hunyuan ready template ships in `vibecomfy/ready_templates/video/`. Owner: VibeComfy maintainer; target: Sprint 4.
   - Any further gaps surfaced by Step 3 §2 mapping table get the same explicit go/no-go gate per cohort (e.g., `qwen_image_hires`, `annotated_image_edit` — executor verifies during write-up).
5. Specify **vendor-utility shims**: RIFE keeps vendored under `reigh-worker/source/media/`; Uni3C as VibeComfy patch on Wan2.2 templates or load directly inside handler; Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow pre-process before workflow build (preferred); Qwen prompt expander pre-process; LoRA-key sanitizer becomes a VibeComfy patch over `LoraLoader` nodes.
6. Specify **dynamic-model-definition** decision: recommend build-time freeze (ship templates upfront), call out as Open Question Q1.
7. **Decide the fate of `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` + `comfy_utils.py`** (must be settled before Sprint 2 work): proposal is to **retire** `ComfyUIManager`/`ComfyUIClient` (delegated to `vibecomfy.runtime.session.EmbeddedSession` and `vibecomfy.runtime.client.ComfyClient`), **keep** the `comfy` task-type dispatch in `task_registry.py:1507` but rewrite `handle_comfy_task` to submit through the new VibeComfy backend. Replace the proposed parallel files (`source/models/comfy/template_routing.py`, `source/models/comfy/comfy_backend.py`) with: refactor of the existing `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` plus a new sibling `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` (only file genuinely added). Tests under `tests/` that import the existing modules must be migrated — listed explicitly in the doc.
8. Specify **observability shim**: translate `RunResult` (`run_id`, `prompt_id`, `outputs`, `metadata_path`, `log_path`) into existing telemetry (heartbeat logs, `system_logs` table, `source/core/log/debug_card.py`).

### Step 5: Sprint-by-sprint migration plan (Section 4)
**Scope:** Large
1. Sprint table (≤2 wk each), each listing: goals, shippable artifacts, exit criteria, owner, risk, rollback move.
   - **Sprint 0 (1 wk)** Discovery freeze + benchmark harness via `scripts/run_worker_matrix.py` + `scripts/live_test/`. Output: `reigh-worker/docs/migration-baselines.md`. Also produce the live task-surface inventory from `task_registry.py:1442-1511` and `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`, and the live VibeComfy template inventory (`find vibecomfy/ready_templates -name '*.py'`).
   - **Sprint 1 (2 wk)** VibeComfy memory-profile MVP (Section 3 §2): `MemoryProfile` enum + `SessionConfig` overlay + per-call `override_profile`. Exit: profiles 1–5 round-trip on `image/z_image` and `video/wan_t2v` with measured VRAM/wall-clock at parity-or-better than `--wgp-profile {1..5}`.
   - **Sprint 2 (2 wk)** Adapter shim in reigh-worker — **refactor existing `source/models/comfy/`** rather than parallel create:
     - Rewrite `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` to call VibeComfy via `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`.
     - Add `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` (task_type → template_id + parameter adapter) covering both the direct-queue path and child tasks enqueued by orchestrated handlers.
     - Retire `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` (`ComfyUIManager`/`ComfyUIClient`); migrate any test imports.
     - Feature flag `REIGH_BACKEND={wgp|comfy}` read in `task_queue.py`/`wgp_init.py` and threaded through to **both** seam (a) (direct queue: `_handle_direct_queue_task`) and seam (b) (nested handler-created child tasks via `context["task_queue"]`).
     - Wire safest tasks first: `z_image_turbo`, `qwen_image_2512`, `t2v`. Exit: feature-flagged Comfy path runs end-to-end in dev for those three, including a smoke run through a `travel_segment` orchestrator that enqueues a `t2v` child task.
   - **Sprint 3 (2 wk)** Dual-execution + comparison harness `scripts/dual_run_compare.py` (image hash, video frame-pHash, frame count, dimensions, audio length, latency, VRAM, OOM count). Nightly across the Sprint 0 matrix. Exit: green report ≥80% of task types.
   - **Sprint 4 (2 wk)** Wan-family + Hunyuan parity. Wan: `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`. **Hunyuan (P0 for this sprint, no go without it):** ship a `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` template in VibeComfy, including memory-profile mapping. Exit: dual-run parity green for Wan family + Hunyuan template runnable with each profile.
   - **Sprint 5 (2 wk)** LTX, Flux, Qwen parity + move pre-processing pipeline (Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow). Exit: dual-run parity green for remaining direct-queue tasks.
   - **Sprint 6 (2 wk)** Orchestrated handlers covering both seams: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`. Exit: full task-type catalog runnable through Comfy backend on both adapter seams.
   - **Sprint 7 (2 wk)** Canary in production by `task_type` cohort using server-side selector (DB column or env). Hold each cohort 48h before promoting next.
   - **Sprint 8 (1 wk)** Wan2GP removal: delete `Wan2GP/`, `source/runtime/wgp_*`, `source/models/wgp/`, WGP-override block in `server.py:544-595`, `--wgp-*` CLI flags (alias `--vibecomfy-profile`); update `worker_startup.template.sh:463`; drop `mmgp` from `pyproject.toml`; revisit `disk_size_gb`.
2. Critical path callout: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3, then 4/5 in parallel, then 6 → 7 → 8.

### Step 6: Per-task-type cutover order with rationale (Section 5)
**Scope:** Small
1. Cohort table covering the **full union task surface** (catalog + dispatch handlers + `comfy`):
   - **A** lowest risk, image-only deterministic: `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_2512`, `flux`, `wan_2_2_t2i`.
   - **B** medium, image edits: `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`.
   - **C** medium-high, video gen: `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`, `generate_video`.
   - **D** high, VACE + Hunyuan: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `hunyuan` — **gated on Hunyuan ready_template existing in `vibecomfy/ready_templates/video/`**.
   - **E** highest, orchestrated + raw-Comfy: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
2. Canary mechanism: server-side `backend_for_task_type` map read at task claim time, with explicit "legacy" fallback while migrating each cohort. Note: orchestrator-dispatched cohort E tasks must respect both the parent task's backend selection AND the child-task seam — covered by Sprint 6.

### Step 7: Rollback plan, telemetry/observability, final removal (Sections 6–8)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Rollback: `REIGH_BACKEND` flag at worker process level via `--backend wgp|comfy` plus per-task-type override; both stacks remain in worker image until Sprint 8; orchestrator passes flag through `worker_startup.template.sh`. Trigger conditions: latency > p95 baseline by N%, new error-class spike, output-divergence rate > threshold. Document signals (`heartbeat_guardian` error classes, `system_logs` filters, dual-run delta thresholds). Adapter scope explicitly covers both seams: direct-queue conversion (`_handle_direct_queue_task`) and nested handler-created child tasks routed via `context["task_queue"]`.
2. Telemetry: add `backend` and `template_id` log labels; emit `vibecomfy.run_id` and `comfy.prompt_id` alongside task id; mirror VRAM logging from `wgp/generators/output.py:log_memory_stats` to Comfy path; capture `RunResult.log_path` into `source/core/log/debug_card.py`.
3. Final Wan2GP removal checklist: every file/dir/commit move (Sprint 8), explicitly enumerated for **both repos**:
   - reigh-worker: `Wan2GP/` submodule, `source/runtime/wgp_*`, `source/models/wgp/`, WGP-override block at `source/runtime/worker/server.py:544-595`, `--wgp-*` CLI flags (rename to `--vibecomfy-profile`), `mmgp==3.7.6` from `pyproject.toml` and `uv.lock`, `tests/test_wgp_patch_lifecycle.py` mmgp shims, retired `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` if replaced.
   - reigh-worker-orchestrator: `gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:463` (`--wgp-profile 1` → `--vibecomfy-profile 1`), `gpu_orchestrator/Dockerfile` (drop Wan2GP install), `requirements.txt`/`requirements-dev.txt` (drop mmgp transitively), env example updates.

### Step 8: Open questions, assumptions, risks/mitigations (Section 9)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open questions:
   - Q1 Static vs dynamic VibeComfy template authoring (mirror of `load_missing_model_definition`)?
   - Q2 Does ComfyUI's per-process memory model accept a 5-tier profile mapping cleanly via `SessionConfig` overrides, or do some profile transitions require a Comfy process restart? Determines whether per-call `override_profile` is feasible without queue-flush.
   - Q3 Output bit-exact reproduction is unrealistic — what divergence threshold (frame-pHash distance, perceptual delta, audio length tolerance) defines "pass" in dual-run harness?
   - Q4 Pre-process annotators host: `reigh-worker` or VibeComfy plugins under `vibecomfy_extras/`?
   - Q5 RunPod startup-time differences (image size, ComfyUI cold-boot) significant enough to change worker amortization or `disk_size_gb`?
   - Q6 LoRA-key sanitizer (`wgp_patches.py:389-445`) — mmgp-specific or does ComfyUI's LoRA loader trip on the same key shapes?
   - Q7 RIFE / Uni3C ControlNet — keep vendored in reigh-worker or wrap as VibeComfy custom nodes?
   - Q8 Hunyuan ready_template authorship: who builds it, against which reference workflow, and on what timeline (Sprint 4 hard gate)?
   - Q9 Is the existing `comfy` task type still externally needed after migration, or can it be retired now that all backends route through VibeComfy?
2. Assumptions: queue contracts and Supabase schema unchanged; orchestrator continues single-GPU pods; `reigh-app` unaffected; `vibecomfy` repo is canonical home for new templates; the 5-tier display contract (`server.py:605-609`) is preserved as public output.
3. Risks + mitigations: missing 1–5 profile tier (Sprint 1 P0, layered on `SessionConfig`); embedded ComfyUI cold-boot dominating short-job latency (long-lived `EmbeddedSession`); template catalog drift (template-routing tests + live-filesystem inventory); Hunyuan template absence (Sprint 4 hard gate); LoRA divergence (golden-output corpus); pod disk too small during dual-run (Sprint 0 baselining + bump `disk_size_gb`); orchestrated child-task seam missed by adapter (Sprint 2 explicit dual-seam wiring + Sprint 6 coverage); existing `source/models/comfy` integration drift (Sprint 2 audit + retire/refactor decision).

### Step 9: Self-review checklist before handoff
**Scope:** Small
1. Verify exactly one H1, TOC IDs resolve, every referenced file path resolves under the working tree.
2. Verify the audit table in Step 2 §3 references **every** task type from the union of `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` ∪ `task_registry.py:1442-1511` dispatch keys, AND the cutover cohort table in Step 6 references the same union. Names match runtime values (`rife_interpolate_images`, not `rife_interpolate`).
3. Verify the Hunyuan template absence is identified as a hard gate for Cohort D / Sprint 4.
4. Verify the memory-profile abstraction is described as layering on `SessionConfig.{vram_policy,reserve_vram_gb,cache_policy,disable_smart_memory}` (citations to `session.py:36-52,625-678`).
5. Verify the existing `source/models/comfy/` integration (handler + utils + dispatch + tests) has an explicit reuse/extend/retire decision.
6. Verify the adapter scope explicitly covers both the direct-queue conversion seam and the nested handler-child-task seam.
7. Confirm no unsourced claims; each capability gap cites either a file path or a documented absence.

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1 → 2 → 3 in order (frame, then audit each side independently).
2. Step 4 depends on the gap rows surfaced by 2+3 and on the existing-Comfy decision (§7).
3. Steps 5, 6, 7 build on 4 and may be drafted in parallel.
4. Step 8 collected throughout, finalized last.
5. Step 9 final pass before handing back to executor.

## Validation Order
1. Executor writes `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` per this plan.
2. Reviewer verifies (a) file exists at exactly `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`; (b) every Step's required table/section is present with named columns; (c) every task type from `task_types.TASK_TYPE_CATALOG` ∪ dispatch keys in `task_registry.py:1442-1511` (`travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`) is referenced ≥1 time; (d) `rife_interpolate_images` (not `rife_interpolate`) is the runtime name used; (e) memory-profile gap is identified as P0 and described as layering on existing `SessionConfig` knobs; (f) Hunyuan template absence is a hard gate for its cohort; (g) existing `source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}` has an explicit reuse/extend/retire decision; (h) adapter scope explicitly covers both direct-queue conversion AND nested handler child-task enqueue paths; (i) sprints ≤2 weeks each and sequenced for incremental risk retirement; (j) rollback mechanism is concrete (named env flag + per-task override); (k) all source-code references resolve.

  "assumptions": [
    "Queue contracts (Supabase task table schema, claim/status update flow, output upload paths) remain unchanged across the migration \u2014 only the in-process generation backend swaps.",
    "`reigh-worker-orchestrator` continues to provision single-GPU RunPod pods using its current spawner; the migration changes only the worker startup command and image, not the orchestration algorithm.",
    "`reigh-app` (frontend + edge functions) is unaffected; existing per-task UI controls map onto the new backend without API changes.",
    "VibeComfy at `./vibecomfy` is the canonical home for new templates and patches; reigh-worker depends on it as a Python package and does not fork its templates.",
    "The 5-tier memory-profile semantics (Max Performance / High RAM / Balanced / Conservative / Minimum) are preserved as the public contract; display strings in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:605-609` continue to surface in the worker UI/logs even after the underlying mechanism changes to `SessionConfig` overlays.",
    "The proposed `MemoryProfile`\u2192`SessionConfig` mapping is a starting hypothesis; final values come from Sprint 1 measurement against the Wan2GP profile baseline.",
    "Dual-running both backends temporarily increases pod disk requirements; we will revisit `disk_size_gb` (currently 50GB and already tight per project memory) during Sprint 0 baselining.",
    "Bit-exact output reproduction is not required; perceptual/structural equivalence within an agreed threshold is acceptable for cutover.",
    "Pre-processing helpers (Canny, DepthV2, Pose, Flow, RIFE, Uni3C, Qwen prompt expander) can be invoked from reigh-worker before the VibeComfy generation call rather than embedded inside ComfyUI workflows, unless a specific case requires graph-internal placement.",
    "The existing `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` + `comfy_utils.py` are safe to refactor (and `comfy_utils.py` to retire) because they are reachable only through the `comfy` dispatch branch at `task_registry.py:1507` and tests that can be migrated.",
    "The runtime task-type identifier is `rife_interpolate_images`; the friendly alias `rife_interpolate` from `display_names.py:33` is presentation-only and must not appear in routing or cutover plans.",
    "Plan-phase output is the structured markdown below; the executor phase will be the only writer to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`."
  ],
  "delta_from_previous_percent": 53.91,
  "structure_warnings": []

    "idea": "Produce a migration plan document for moving reigh-worker's execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy. Repos live side-by-side at ./reigh-worker (current Wan2GP consumer) and ./vibecomfy (target). Plan must: (1) Audit current reigh-worker->Wan2GP integration: every task type, memory-profile system (lowvram/medvram/highvram/profiled/etc.), model load/unload lifecycle, queue/dispatch shape, error paths, telemetry, worker-orchestrator coupling. (2) Audit VibeComfy capabilities: ready_templates, VibeWorkflow IR, validation, embedded local execution, RunPod execution path, gaps vs Wan2GP (especially memory profiles). (3) Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (memory-profile abstraction modeled on Wan2GP's, equivalents for every task type, model-management lifecycle). (4) Sprint-by-sprint migration plan, each sprint <=2 weeks, sequenced for incremental risk retirement (shadow/dual-run/canary before full cutover), with concrete shippable outcomes. Cover: VibeComfy parity work, adapter/shim in reigh-worker, dual-execution+comparison harness, per-task-type cutover order with rationale, rollback plan, telemetry/observability changes, final Wan2GP removal. (5) Open questions, assumptions, decision points, risks, mitigations. Context: reigh-worker uses Wan2GP today as in-process execution backend across multiple task types (image gen, video gen, edits, etc.). Memory profiles are non-negotiable \u2014 full parity required before cutover. VibeComfy is a Python toolkit around ComfyUI workflows with VibeWorkflow IR, ready_templates, embedded-local + RunPod execution. Migration should preserve reigh-worker's external behavior (queue contracts, output shapes, latency SLOs). Orchestrator (./reigh-worker-orchestrator) provisions GPU workers; coordinate worker-image/runtime changes with that. Output: docs/migration-vibecomfy.md.",

Strengthened Step 9 self-review and the validation gate to enforce the union task surface, runtime task-type names, the Hunyuan gate, the SessionConfig layering, and the existing-Comfy decision.",
    "category": "correctness",

The revised checklist only names the `--wgp-profile` argument at line 463 plus Dockerfile/requirements.",

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",

      ]
