Document explicit gaps: no 1–5 profile tier abstraction (only the lower-level knobs in §4); no LoRA-key sanitizer equivalent; no Uni3C ControlNet cache; no RIFE temporal interpolation helper; no Qwen prompt expander wrapper; no Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotator re-exports; no Wan2GP `save_video` callable; no Hunyuan ready_template.

### Step 4: Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (Section 3)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Author "Parity Gap Matrix" table (cols: capability, current Wan2GP location, VibeComfy current state, required work, P-priority, owner-repo, target sprint).
2. Specify **memory-profile abstraction (P0)** as a thin overlay on existing `SessionConfig` knobs (Step 3 §4). New `vibecomfy.runtime.profile` module with `MemoryProfile` enum (1–5) and `MemoryProfile.to_session_overrides()` returning a partial dict overlaying `vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`. Both `_embedded_configuration_for_session` (`session.py:625-656`) and `_comfy_server_argv` (`session.py:663-678`) consume the resulting `SessionConfig` unchanged. Concrete starting mapping (Sprint-1 tuned): MAX_PERFORMANCE → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; HIGH_RAM → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="lru:32"`; BALANCED → `vram_policy="normal"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; CONSERVATIVE → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="classic"`, `reserve_vram_gb=2.0`; MINIMUM → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="none"`, `disable_smart_memory=True`, `reserve_vram_gb=4.0`. Per-call `override_profile` mirrors `wgp_params.py` semantics. Acceptance: per-profile parity smoke tests against profile-1 (prod) and profile-3 (dev) baselines.
3. Specify **task-type → VibeComfy template registry**: lives at `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` (only genuinely new file). Covers the union task surface — direct-queue catalog plus orchestrated-handler internal sub-tasks.
4. **Missing-template gaps with explicit P-priority and go/no-go**:
   - **Hunyuan (P0 for Cohort D / Sprint 4)** — no `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` exists. Cohort D blocked until template ships. Owner: VibeComfy maintainer; target: Sprint 4.
   - Any further gaps from Step 3 §2 mapping table get explicit go/no-go gate per cohort.
5. **Vendor-utility shims**: RIFE keeps vendored under `reigh-worker/source/media/`; Uni3C as VibeComfy patch on Wan2.2 templates; Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow pre-process before workflow build; Qwen prompt expander pre-process; LoRA-key sanitizer becomes a VibeComfy patch over `LoraLoader` nodes.
6. **Dynamic-model-definition** decision: recommend build-time freeze; Open Question Q1.
7. **Existing `source/models/comfy/` decision**: refactor `comfy_handler.py` to delegate via `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; retire `comfy_utils.py`; add `template_routing.py` (only new file). Migrate test imports.
8. **Observability shim**: translate `RunResult` (`run_id`, `prompt_id`, `outputs`, `metadata_path`, `log_path`) into existing telemetry (heartbeat logs, `system_logs`, `source/core/log/debug_card.py`).

### Step 5: Sprint-by-sprint migration plan (Section 4)
**Scope:** Large
1. Sprint table (≤2 wk each), each listing: goals, shippable artifacts, exit criteria, owner, risk, rollback move.
   - **Sprint 0 (1 wk)** Discovery freeze + benchmark harness via `scripts/run_worker_matrix.py` + `scripts/live_test/`. Output: `reigh-worker/docs/migration-baselines.md` with separate baselines for prod profile-1 and dev profile-3. Live task-surface inventory (`task_registry.py:1442-1511` ∪ `TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`) and live VibeComfy template inventory.
   - **Sprint 1 (2 wk)** VibeComfy memory-profile MVP. Exit: profiles 1–5 round-trip on `image/z_image` and `video/wan_t2v` with measured VRAM/wall-clock at parity-or-better than `--wgp-profile {1..5}` for both prod-profile-1 and dev-profile-3 reference points.
   - **Sprint 2 (2 wk)** Adapter shim — refactor existing `source/models/comfy/`. Rewrite `comfy_handler.py` to call `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; add `template_routing.py`; retire `comfy_utils.py`; migrate test imports. Feature flag `REIGH_BACKEND={wgp|comfy}` threaded through both seams (`_handle_direct_queue_task` and `context["task_queue"]`). Wire safest tasks first: `z_image_turbo`, `qwen_image_2512`, `t2v`. Exit: feature-flagged Comfy path runs end-to-end in dev plus a `travel_segment` smoke run that enqueues a `t2v` child.
   - **Sprint 3 (2 wk)** Dual-execution + comparison harness `scripts/dual_run_compare.py` (image hash, video frame-pHash, frame count, dimensions, audio length, latency, VRAM, OOM count). Nightly across the Sprint 0 matrix. Exit: green report ≥80% of task types.
   - **Sprint 4 (2 wk)** Wan-family + **Hunyuan (P0 hard gate)**: ship `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` with memory-profile mapping. Wan: `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`. Exit: dual-run parity green for Wan family + Hunyuan template runnable with each profile.
   - **Sprint 5 (2 wk)** LTX, Flux, Qwen parity + move pre-processing pipeline. Exit: dual-run parity green for remaining direct-queue tasks.
   - **Sprint 6 (2 wk)** Orchestrated handlers covering both seams: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`. Exit: full union task surface runnable through Comfy backend on both adapter seams.
   - **Sprint 7 (2 wk)** Canary in production by `task_type` cohort using server-side selector. Hold each cohort 48 h before promoting next.
   - **Sprint 8 (1 wk)** Wan2GP removal (full enumeration in Step 7 §3, plus mandatory `git grep` sweep at Step 9 §10).
2. Critical path callout: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3, then 4/5 in parallel, then 6 → 7 → 8.

### Step 6: Per-task-type cutover order with rationale (Section 5)
**Scope:** Small
1. Cohort table covering the union task surface:
   - **A** lowest risk, image-only deterministic: `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_2512`, `flux`, `wan_2_2_t2i`.
   - **B** medium, image edits: `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`.
   - **C** medium-high, video gen: `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`, `generate_video`.
   - **D** high, VACE + Hunyuan: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `hunyuan` — gated on Hunyuan ready_template.
   - **E** highest, orchestrated + raw-Comfy: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
2. Canary mechanism: server-side `backend_for_task_type` map read at task claim time. Cohort E respects parent backend selection AND the child-task seam (Sprint 6).

### Step 7: Rollback plan, telemetry/observability, final removal (Sections 6–8)
**Scope:** Medium

Rollback: `REIGH_BACKEND` flag (process level via `--backend wgp|comfy`) plus per-task-type override; both stacks coexist in the worker image until Sprint 8; orchestrator passes flag through `worker_startup.template.sh`. Trigger conditions: latency > p95 baseline by N%, error-class spike, output-divergence rate > threshold. Adapter scope explicitly covers both seams.

Plus any architecture/coverage tests that import retired WGP surfaces (surfaced by the `git grep` sweep, see Step 9 §10).
   - **reigh-worker existing-Comfy refactor residue:** `source/models/comfy/comfy_utils.py` (already retired in Sprint 2; verify removed).

### Step 8: Open questions, assumptions, risks/mitigations (Section 9)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open questions: Q1 static vs dynamic VibeComfy template authoring; Q2 5-tier `MemoryProfile`→`SessionConfig` overlay process-restart feasibility; Q3 dual-run divergence threshold; Q4 preprocessing annotators in `reigh-worker` vs `vibecomfy_extras/`; Q5 RunPod startup-time / `disk_size_gb` impact; Q6 LoRA-key sanitizer mmgp-specific vs ComfyUI portable; Q7 RIFE / Uni3C vendor location; Q8 Hunyuan ready_template ownership; Q9 fate of `comfy` task type; Q10 dev/prod default-profile alignment; **Q11** whether `headless_model_management` (separate from `headless_wgp`) has any non-WGP callers we need to migrate vs delete.
2. Assumptions: queue contracts and Supabase schema unchanged; `reigh-app` unaffected; `vibecomfy` is canonical home for new templates; the 5-tier display contract is preserved; default profile diverges by env (prod=1, dev=3); both `pyproject.toml` `headless_wgp` entries (lines 109 and 165) are duplicate registrations (one in `[project.scripts]`, one likely in `[tool.poetry.scripts]` or section block) and both must be removed.
3. Risks + mitigations: missing 1–5 profile tier (Sprint 1 P0); embedded ComfyUI cold-boot (long-lived `EmbeddedSession`); template catalog drift (template-routing tests); Hunyuan template absence (Sprint 4 hard gate); LoRA divergence (golden-output corpus); pod disk too small during dual-run (Sprint 0 baseline); orchestrated child-task seam missed by adapter (Sprint 2 dual-seam wiring); existing `source/models/comfy` integration drift (Sprint 2 retire/refactor); orchestrator startup-script Wan2GP coupling missed at cleanup (Step 7 §3 enumeration); **residual WGP entrypoints/tests left after directory deletion (Step 7 §3 explicit per-file list + Step 9 §10 mandatory `git grep` sweep).**

  "questions": [
    "Q1: Should reigh-worker freeze the VibeComfy template catalog at build time, or preserve runtime model-definition injection (analogue of `load_missing_model_definition`)?",
    "Q2: Does ComfyUI's per-process memory model accept the proposed 5-tier `MemoryProfile`\u2192`SessionConfig` overlay cleanly, or do some profile transitions require a Comfy process restart?",
    "Q3: Bit-exact output reproduction across backends is unrealistic \u2014 what is the acceptable divergence threshold (frame-pHash distance, perceptual delta, audio length tolerance) for dual-run pass/fail?",
    "Q4: Should preprocessing annotators (Canny, DepthV2, Pose, Flow) live in reigh-worker (called before VibeComfy) or be migrated as VibeComfy plugins under `vibecomfy_extras/`?",
    "Q5: Are there RunPod startup-time differences (image size, ComfyUI cold-boot) significant enough to change worker amortization economics or `disk_size_gb` defaults?",
    "Q6: Is the LoRA-key sanitizer in `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py:389-445` mmgp-specific, or does ComfyUI's LoRA loader trip on the same key shapes?",
    "Q7: Should we keep RIFE temporal interpolation vendored inside reigh-worker (as a `source/media/` helper) or wrap it as a VibeComfy custom node? Same for Uni3C ControlNet cache.",
    "Q8: Hunyuan ready_template authorship: who builds it, against which reference workflow, and on what timeline? (Hard gate for Cohort D / Sprint 4.)",
    "Q9: After the migration, should the existing `comfy` task type be retired, kept as a thin pass-through for raw-API workflows, or repurposed as the canonical \u201cescape hatch\u201d for hand-authored ComfyUI JSON?",
    "Q10: Should the dev default profile (currently 3 in `start_worker.bat:14` and `scripts/live_test/{main,smoke}.py:27`) be aligned with the production default (1 in `worker_startup.template.sh:463`) before cutover?",
    "Q11: Does `reigh-worker/headless_model_management.py` (and its entrypoint shim at `source/runtime/entrypoints/headless_model_management.py`) have any non-WGP callers that need migration to a VibeComfy-equivalent CLI, or can it be deleted outright with `headless_wgp` in Sprint 8?",
    "Q12: Are the duplicate `headless_wgp` console-script entries at `pyproject.toml:109` and `pyproject.toml:165` a known artifact (e.g., one in `[project.scripts]` and one in `[tool.poetry.scripts]`/`[project.entry-points]`), or a copy-paste bug to clean up independently before Sprint 8?"
  ],

  "assumptions": [
    "Queue contracts (Supabase task table schema, claim/status update flow, output upload paths) remain unchanged across the migration \u2014 only the in-process generation backend swaps.",
    "`reigh-worker-orchestrator` continues to provision single-GPU RunPod pods using its current spawner; the migration changes only the worker startup command, image, and submodule layout.",
    "`reigh-app` (frontend + edge functions) is unaffected; existing per-task UI controls map onto the new backend without API changes.",
    "VibeComfy at `./vibecomfy` is the canonical home for new templates and patches.",
    "The 5-tier memory-profile semantics (Max Performance / High RAM / Balanced / Conservative / Minimum) are preserved as the public contract; display strings in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:605-609` continue to surface in the worker UI/logs even after the underlying mechanism changes to `SessionConfig` overlays.",
    "The proposed `MemoryProfile`\u2192`SessionConfig` mapping is a starting hypothesis; final values come from Sprint 1 measurement against profile-1 (prod) and profile-3 (dev) baselines.",
    "Default profile diverges by environment today (prod=1, dev=3); Sprint 0 measures both, Q10 tracks alignment.",
    "Dual-running both backends temporarily increases pod disk requirements; Sprint 0 baseline revisits `disk_size_gb`.",
    "Bit-exact output reproduction is not required; perceptual/structural equivalence within an agreed threshold is acceptable for cutover.",
    "Pre-processing helpers (Canny, DepthV2, Pose, Flow, RIFE, Uni3C, Qwen prompt expander) can be invoked from reigh-worker before the VibeComfy generation call.",
    "The existing `source/models/comfy/comfy_handler.py` + `comfy_utils.py` are safe to refactor (and `comfy_utils.py` to retire) because they are reachable only through the `comfy` dispatch branch at `task_registry.py:1507` and tests that can be migrated.",
    "The runtime task-type identifier is `rife_interpolate_images`; `rife_interpolate` from `display_names.py:33` is presentation-only.",
    "Both `headless_wgp` console-script registrations at `pyproject.toml:109` and `pyproject.toml:165` are duplicate entries (likely across `[project.scripts]` and a tool-specific section) and both must be deleted; if they turn out to live in conflicting sections the executor reconciles to a single removal.",
    "The full reigh-worker WGP file surface for Sprint 8 is the union of (a) the explicit list in Step 7 \u00a73 and (b) any additional files surfaced by the Step 9 \u00a710 `git grep` sweep; both must be removed before Sprint 8 closes.",
    "Orchestrator-side Wan2GP cleanup at Sprint 8 covers all three touchpoints in `worker_startup.template.sh` (line 174 FALLBACK_DIR, lines 267-292 submodule block, line 463 --wgp-profile) plus Dockerfile and requirements.",
    "Plan-phase output is the structured markdown below; the executor phase will be the only writer to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`."
  ],

        Gate signals:
        {
  "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 4,
    "idea": "Produce a migration plan document for moving reigh-worker's execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy. Repos live side-by-side at ./reigh-worker (current Wan2GP consumer) and ./vibecomfy (target). Plan must: (1) Audit current reigh-worker->Wan2GP integration: every task type, memory-profile system (lowvram/medvram/highvram/profiled/etc.), model load/unload lifecycle, queue/dispatch shape, error paths, telemetry, worker-orchestrator coupling. (2) Audit VibeComfy capabilities: ready_templates, VibeWorkflow IR, validation, embedded local execution, RunPod execution path, gaps vs Wan2GP (especially memory profiles). (3) Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (memory-profile abstraction modeled on Wan2GP's, equivalents for every task type, model-management lifecycle). (4) Sprint-by-sprint migration plan, each sprint <=2 weeks, sequenced for incremental risk retirement (shadow/dual-run/canary before full cutover), with concrete shippable outcomes. Cover: VibeComfy parity work, adapter/shim in reigh-worker, dual-execution+comparison harness, per-task-type cutover order with rationale, rollback plan, telemetry/observability changes, final Wan2GP removal. (5) Open questions, assumptions, decision points, risks, mitigations. Context: reigh-worker uses Wan2GP today as in-process execution backend across multiple task types (image gen, video gen, edits, etc.). Memory profiles are non-negotiable \u2014 full parity required before cutover. VibeComfy is a Python toolkit around ComfyUI workflows with VibeWorkflow IR, ready_templates, embedded-local + RunPod execution. Migration should preserve reigh-worker's external behavior (queue contracts, output shapes, latency SLOs). Orchestrator (./reigh-worker-orchestrator) provisions GPU workers; coordinate worker-image/runtime changes with that. Output: docs/migration-vibecomfy.md.",

        Known accepted debt grouped by subsystem:
        {
  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-145",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"

    }

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

  {
    "subsystem": "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements",
    "total_occurrences": 3,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "subsystem": "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure",
    "total_occurrences": 3,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-014",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",

        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-110",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Migrate reigh-worker execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy

## Overview
Author a migration plan document at `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md` covering the cutover of `reigh-worker`'s in-process generation backend from vendored `Wan2GP/wgp.py` (orchestrated via `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`) to `vibecomfy` (Python toolkit around ComfyUI, with `VibeWorkflow` IR, `ready_templates/`, embedded + RunPod runtimes in `vibecomfy/runtime/`).

Key constraints discovered while scoping (verified against the working tree):
- **Memory profiles** are non-negotiable. Today: `--wgp-profile {1..5}` in `source/runtime/worker/server.py:556-566` mapping to mmgp's `force_profile_no` / `default_profile`, plus per-task `override_profile` in `source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py:166,237,374`. VibeComfy already exposes lower-level memory controls in `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:46-49` (`SessionConfig.vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`). Translation paths verified: `_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` builds the embedded ComfyUI `Configuration`; `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678` builds the managed-server CLI argv. The 1–5 tier abstraction is missing — it must layer on top of the existing `SessionConfig` surface.
- **Default profile is environment-specific.** Production = `--wgp-profile 1` via `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:463`. Dev = `--wgp-profile 3` via `start_worker.bat:14`, `scripts/live_test/main.py:27`, `scripts/live_test/smoke.py:27`. Sprint 0 baselines both.
- **Task surface to preserve** is the union of `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`, the 14 dispatch keys at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:1442-1511` (`travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `magic_edit`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`), and friendly aliases at `source/core/log/display_names.py:8-39` (`rife_interpolate` is alias-only; runtime name is `rife_interpolate_images`).
- **Existing ComfyUI integration** at `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}` is refactored (not duplicated): `comfy_handler.py` rewritten to delegate to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; `comfy_utils.py` retired; new sibling `source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` is the only added file.
- **Coupling beyond generation**: `WanOrchestrator` handles dynamic model definition load, LoRA setup, Uni3C ControlNet caching, RIFE temporal interpolation, Qwen prompt expander, Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotators, save-video helpers — re-exported through `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` and `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`.
- **Wan2GP surface inside reigh-worker is broader than the wgp_* directories.** Verified inventory: root scripts `reigh-worker/headless_wgp.py` and `reigh-worker/headless_model_management.py`; entrypoint shims `source/runtime/entrypoints/headless_wgp.py` and `source/runtime/entrypoints/headless_model_management.py`; duplicate console-script registrations at `reigh-worker/pyproject.toml:109` and `:165` (both `headless_wgp = "source.runtime.entrypoints.headless_wgp:WanOrchestrator"`); WGP-specific test suite — 7 files: `test_wgp_bridge_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_bridge_ports_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_init_bootstrap_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_output_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_params_overrides.py`, `test_wgp_patch_context_contracts.py`, `test_wgp_patch_lifecycle.py`.
- **Orchestrator coupling extends beyond `--wgp-profile`**: `worker_startup.template.sh` references Wan2GP at line 174 + 179-183 (`FALLBACK_DIR="$WORKSPACE_DIR/Headless-Wan2GP"`), lines 267-292 (Wan2GP submodule reconciliation), and line 463 (`--wgp-profile 1`).
- **VibeComfy ready templates** at `vibecomfy/ready_templates/{image,video,edit,audio}/` count **50 `.py` files** (live `find`; README's "46" is stale). No Hunyuan template — P0 hard gate for Cohort D.
- **Repository layout for closure sweeps:** `reigh-worker/`, `reigh-worker-orchestrator/`, and `vibecomfy/` are **independent Git repositories** nested inside the workspace (per user-provided memory and recent commit history). Any closure-sweep tool that respects Git boundaries must be invoked **per repo** (`git -C <repo> grep …`), or use a non-Git traversal tool such as `rg`. A workspace-root `git grep` returns no hits and would silently pass.

The deliverable is a planning markdown doc; no code changes in this phase. The executor will write to `docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Frame the document and write metadata header (`docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open with H1 `# reigh-worker → VibeComfy Migration Plan`, status banner ("Draft, 2026-05-05"), one-paragraph summary, and a clickable TOC.
2. State goals (preserve external worker contract: queue payloads, output shapes, per-task SLOs, memory-profile guarantees) and explicit non-goals (no Supabase queue schema rework, no orchestrator algorithmic changes beyond image/runtime swap, no `reigh-app` UI/API changes).
3. Reference authoritative source paths: `reigh-worker/source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py`, `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/queue/task_queue.py`, `reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py`, `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/{comfy_handler.py,comfy_utils.py}`, `reigh-worker/source/core/log/display_names.py`, `reigh-worker/headless_wgp.py`, `reigh-worker/headless_model_management.py`, `reigh-worker/source/runtime/entrypoints/{headless_wgp.py,headless_model_management.py}`, `reigh-worker/pyproject.toml:109,165`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/{run.py,session.py,server.py,client.py}`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/`, `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py`, `vibecomfy/docs/runtime_surface.md`.

### Step 2: Audit current reigh-worker → Wan2GP integration (Section 1)
**Scope:** Medium
1. ### Step 3: Audit VibeComfy capabilities (Section 2)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Document `VibeWorkflow` IR (`vibecomfy/vibecomfy/workflow.py`): `add_node`/`connect`/`disconnect`/`replace_edge`/`finalize_metadata`/`compile("api")` per `vibecomfy/docs/authoring.md`. Rule: changes-handles → template, decorates-handles → patch.
2. Document `ready_templates/` organization. Use the live `find vibecomfy/ready_templates -name '*.py' | wc -l` count of **50** (verified) and explicitly note the README's "46" is stale. Map known templates to direct-queue task types. Mark `hunyuan` as a missing-template gap.
3. Document the runtime: `run_embedded`/`run_embedded_sync` (`runtime/run.py`) over `EmbeddedSession` (`runtime/session.py`); `run`/`run_sync` against an external Comfy server (`runtime/{client.py,server.py}`).
4. Document **existing memory controls** on `SessionConfig` (`session.py:46-49`) and the two translation paths:
   - `_embedded_configuration_for_session` at `session.py:625-656` builds the embedded ComfyUI `Configuration` dict (`highvram`/`lowvram`/`normalvram`, `reserve_vram`, `cache_classic`/`cache_none`/`cache_lru`, `disable_smart_memory`, then `config.extra` and `VIBECOMFY_COMFY_CONFIGURATION` env JSON merge).
   - `_comfy_server_argv` at `session.py:663-678` builds the managed-server CLI argv (`--highvram`/`--lowvram`/`--normalvram`, `--reserve-vram`, `--disable-smart-memory`, `--cache-classic`/`--cache-none`/`--cache-lru N`, `--port`).
5. Document validation: `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/schema/{validate.py,cache.py,format.py,provider.py}` plus `vibecomfy doctor`.
6. Document RunPod path: `runpod-lifecycle` via `vibecomfy/vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py`. For reigh-worker, `EmbeddedSession` is the analogue of vendored `wgp.py`.
7. Document explicit gaps: no 1–5 profile tier abstraction (only the lower-level knobs in §4); no LoRA-key sanitizer equivalent; no Uni3C ControlNet cache; no RIFE temporal interpolation helper; no Qwen prompt expander wrapper; no Canny/Depth/Flow/Pose annotator re-exports; no Wan2GP `save_video` callable; no Hunyuan ready_template.

### Step 4: Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (Section 3)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Author "Parity Gap Matrix" table (cols: capability, current Wan2GP location, VibeComfy current state, required work, P-priority, owner-repo, target sprint).
2. Specify **memory-profile abstraction (P0)** as a thin overlay on existing `SessionConfig` knobs (Step 3 §4). New `vibecomfy.runtime.profile` module with `MemoryProfile` enum (1–5) and `MemoryProfile.to_session_overrides()` returning a partial dict overlaying `vram_policy`, `reserve_vram_gb`, `cache_policy`, `disable_smart_memory`. Both `_embedded_configuration_for_session` (`session.py:625-656`) and `_comfy_server_argv` (`session.py:663-678`) consume the resulting `SessionConfig` unchanged. Concrete starting mapping (Sprint-1 tuned): MAX_PERFORMANCE → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; HIGH_RAM → `vram_policy="high"`, `cache_policy="lru:32"`; BALANCED → `vram_policy="normal"`, `cache_policy="smart"`; CONSERVATIVE → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="classic"`, `reserve_vram_gb=2.0`; MINIMUM → `vram_policy="low"`, `cache_policy="none"`, `disable_smart_memory=True`, `reserve_vram_gb=4.0`. Per-call `override_profile` mirrors `wgp_params.py` semantics. Acceptance: per-profile parity smoke tests against profile-1 (prod) and profile-3 (dev) baselines.
3. Specify **task-type → VibeComfy template registry**: lives at `reigh-worker/source/models/comfy/template_routing.py` (only genuinely new file). Covers the union task surface — direct-queue catalog plus orchestrated-handler internal sub-tasks.
4. **Missing-template gaps with explicit P-priority and go/no-go**:
   - **Hunyuan (P0 for Cohort D / Sprint 4)** — no `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` exists. Cohort D blocked until template ships. Owner: VibeComfy maintainer; target: Sprint 4.
   - Any further gaps from Step 3 §2 mapping table get explicit go/no-go gate per cohort.
5. **Vendor-utility shims**: RIFE keeps vendored under `reigh-worker/source/media/`; Uni3C as VibeComfy patch on Wan2.2 templates; Canny/Depth/Pose/Flow pre-process before workflow build; Qwen prompt expander pre-process; LoRA-key sanitizer becomes a VibeComfy patch over `LoraLoader` nodes.
6. **Dynamic-model-definition** decision: recommend build-time freeze; Open Question Q1.
7. **Existing `source/models/comfy/` decision**: refactor `comfy_handler.py` to delegate via `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; retire `comfy_utils.py`; add `template_routing.py` (only new file). Migrate test imports.
8. **Observability shim**: translate `RunResult` (`run_id`, `prompt_id`, `outputs`, `metadata_path`, `log_path`) into existing telemetry (heartbeat logs, `system_logs`, `source/core/log/debug_card.py`).

### Step 5: Sprint-by-sprint migration plan (Section 4)
**Scope:** Large
1. Sprint table (≤2 wk each), each listing: goals, shippable artifacts, exit criteria, owner, risk, rollback move.
   - **Sprint 0 (1 wk)** Discovery freeze + benchmark harness via `scripts/run_worker_matrix.py` + `scripts/live_test/`. Output: `reigh-worker/docs/migration-baselines.md` with separate baselines for prod profile-1 and dev profile-3. Live task-surface inventory (`task_registry.py:1442-1511` ∪ `TASK_TYPE_CATALOG`) and live VibeComfy template inventory.
   - **Sprint 1 (2 wk)** VibeComfy memory-profile MVP. Exit: profiles 1–5 round-trip on `image/z_image` and `video/wan_t2v` with measured VRAM/wall-clock at parity-or-better than `--wgp-profile {1..5}` for both prod-profile-1 and dev-profile-3 reference points.
   - **Sprint 2 (2 wk)** Adapter shim — refactor existing `source/models/comfy/`. Rewrite `comfy_handler.py` to call `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded`; add `template_routing.py`; retire `comfy_utils.py`; migrate test imports. Feature flag `REIGH_BACKEND={wgp|comfy}` threaded through both seams (`_handle_direct_queue_task` and `context["task_queue"]`). Wire safest tasks first: `z_image_turbo`, `qwen_image_2512`, `t2v`. Exit: feature-flagged Comfy path runs end-to-end in dev plus a `travel_segment` smoke run that enqueues a `t2v` child.
   - **Sprint 3 (2 wk)** Dual-execution + comparison harness `scripts/dual_run_compare.py` (image hash, video frame-pHash, frame count, dimensions, audio length, latency, VRAM, OOM count). Nightly across the Sprint 0 matrix. Exit: green report ≥80% of task types.
   - **Sprint 4 (2 wk)** Wan-family + **Hunyuan (P0 hard gate)**: ship `ready_templates/video/hunyuan_*` with memory-profile mapping. Wan: `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `wan_2_2_t2i`, `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`. Exit: dual-run parity green for Wan family + Hunyuan template runnable with each profile.
   - **Sprint 5 (2 wk)** LTX, Flux, Qwen parity + move pre-processing pipeline. Exit: dual-run parity green for remaining direct-queue tasks.
   - **Sprint 6 (2 wk)** Orchestrated handlers covering both seams: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`. Exit: full union task surface runnable through Comfy backend on both adapter seams.
   - **Sprint 7 (2 wk)** Canary in production by `task_type` cohort using server-side selector. Hold each cohort 48 h before promoting next.
   - **Sprint 8 (1 wk)** Wan2GP removal (full enumeration in Step 7 §3, plus mandatory per-repo grep sweep at Step 9 §10).
2. Critical path callout: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3, then 4/5 in parallel, then 6 → 7 → 8.

### Step 6: Per-task-type cutover order with rationale (Section 5)
**Scope:** Small
1. Cohort table covering the union task surface:
   - **A** lowest risk, image-only deterministic: `z_image_turbo`, `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_2512`, `flux`, `wan_2_2_t2i`.
   - **B** medium, image edits: `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_hires`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`.
   - **C** medium-high, video gen: `t2v`, `t2v_22`, `i2v`, `i2v_22`, `ltxv`, `ltx2`, `generate_video`.
   - **D** high, VACE + Hunyuan: `vace`, `vace_21`, `vace_22`, `hunyuan` — gated on Hunyuan ready_template.
   - **E** highest, orchestrated + raw-Comfy: `travel_orchestrator`, `travel_segment`, `individual_travel_segment`, `travel_stitch`, `join_clips_orchestrator`, `join_clips_segment`, `join_final_stitch`, `inpaint_frames`, `magic_edit`, `edit_video_orchestrator`, `create_visualization`, `extract_frame`, `rife_interpolate_images`, `comfy`.
2. Canary mechanism: server-side `backend_for_task_type` map read at task claim time. Cohort E respects parent backend selection AND the child-task seam (Sprint 6).

### Step 7: Rollback plan, telemetry/observability, final removal (Sections 6–8)
**Scope:** Medium

Rollback: `REIGH_BACKEND` flag (process level via `--backend wgp|comfy`) plus per-task-type override; both stacks coexist in the worker image until Sprint 8; orchestrator passes flag through `worker_startup.template.sh`. Trigger conditions: latency > p95 baseline by N%, error-class spike, output-divergence rate > threshold. Adapter scope explicitly covers both seams.

Plus any architecture/coverage tests that import retired WGP surfaces (surfaced by the per-repo grep sweep, see Step 9 §10).

### Step 8: Open questions, assumptions, risks/mitigations (Section 9)
**Scope:** Small
1. Open questions: Q1 static vs dynamic VibeComfy template authoring; Q2 5-tier `MemoryProfile`→`SessionConfig` overlay process-restart feasibility; Q3 dual-run divergence threshold; Q4 preprocessing annotators in `reigh-worker` vs `vibecomfy_extras/`; Q5 RunPod startup-time / `disk_size_gb` impact; Q6 LoRA-key sanitizer mmgp-specific vs ComfyUI portable; Q7 RIFE / Uni3C vendor location; Q8 Hunyuan ready_template ownership; Q9 fate of `comfy` task type; Q10 dev/prod default-profile alignment; Q11 whether `headless_model_management` (separate from `headless_wgp`) has any non-WGP callers we need to migrate vs delete; Q12 whether the duplicate `headless_wgp` entries at `pyproject.toml:109` and `:165` are an intentional cross-section registration or a copy-paste bug.
2. Assumptions: queue contracts and Supabase schema unchanged; `reigh-app` unaffected; `vibecomfy` is canonical home for new templates; the 5-tier display contract is preserved; default profile diverges by env (prod=1, dev=3); both `pyproject.toml` `headless_wgp` entries (lines 109 and 165) are duplicate registrations and both must be removed; **`reigh-worker/`, `reigh-worker-orchestrator/`, and `vibecomfy/` are independent Git repos nested in the workspace, so any closure-sweep tool that respects Git boundaries must be invoked per repo (or use `rg`)**.
3. Risks + mitigations: missing 1–5 profile tier (Sprint 1 P0); embedded ComfyUI cold-boot (long-lived `EmbeddedSession`); template catalog drift (template-routing tests); Hunyuan template absence (Sprint 4 hard gate); LoRA divergence (golden-output corpus); pod disk too small during dual-run (Sprint 0 baseline); orchestrated child-task seam missed by adapter (Sprint 2 dual-seam wiring); existing `source/models/comfy` integration drift (Sprint 2 retire/refactor); orchestrator startup-script Wan2GP coupling missed at cleanup (Step 7 §3 enumeration); residual WGP entrypoints/tests left after directory deletion (Step 7 §3 explicit per-file list + Step 9 §10 mandatory per-repo grep sweep); **closure sweep run from workspace root silently skipping the nested repos (mitigated by Step 9 §10 explicit per-repo `git -C` invocations and `rg` fallback)**.

Use one of these two equivalent verbatim invocations:
    ```bash
    # Option A — Git-aware, respects each repo's tracked-file index (preferred for committed-only sweep):
    git -C reigh-worker grep -lE 'wgp_|headless_wgp|headless_model_management|mmgp|Wan2GP|WanOrchestrator|--wgp-' \
      | sed 's|^|reigh-worker/|'
    git -C reigh-worker-orchestrator grep -lE 'wgp_|headless_wgp|headless_model_management|mmgp|Wan2GP|WanOrchestrator|--wgp-' \
      | sed 's|^|reigh-worker-orchestrator/|'

    # Option B — Filesystem-traversal fallback (catches untracked files; use when verifying post-deletion state):
    rg -l 'wgp_|headless_wgp|headless_model_management|mmgp|Wan2GP|WanOrchestrator|--wgp-' \
      reigh-worker/ reigh-worker-orchestrator/
    ```
    Pre-Sprint-8: run Option A and append any surfaced files (not already in Step 7 §3) to the deletion/migration list. Post-deletion: run Option B and assert it returns zero hits, excluding the migration doc itself (`docs/migration-vibecomfy.md`) and any historical `CHANGELOG`/`docs/` entries explicitly retained for archive (each retained path must be listed in the doc).

  "changes_summary": "Single targeted fix for the v4 sweep-command defect: the workspace-root `git grep -- reigh-worker/ reigh-worker-orchestrator/` returned zero hits because both subdirectories are independent Git repositories (per user memory and recent commit `01d0fc2`). Step 9 \u00a710 explicitly notes that workspace-root `git grep` would silently skip nested repos. Validation Order item (l) updated to require both Option A and Option B and to call out the workspace-root pitfall. The Overview adds a \"Repository layout for closure sweeps\" bullet so the per-repo invariant is anchored upfront, and Step 8 \u00a72/\u00a73 capture the same constraint as a recorded assumption + risk.   "questions": [
    "Q1: Should reigh-worker freeze the VibeComfy template catalog at build time, or preserve runtime model-definition injection?",
    "Q2: Does ComfyUI's per-process memory model accept the proposed 5-tier `MemoryProfile`\u2192`SessionConfig` overlay cleanly, or do some profile transitions require a Comfy process restart?",
    "Q3: What is the acceptable divergence threshold (frame-pHash distance, perceptual delta, audio length tolerance) for dual-run pass/fail?",
    "Q4: Should preprocessing annotators (Canny, DepthV2, Pose, Flow) live in reigh-worker or be migrated as VibeComfy plugins under `vibecomfy_extras/`?",
    "Q5: Are RunPod startup-time differences significant enough to change worker amortization economics or `disk_size_gb` defaults?",
    "Q6: Is the LoRA-key sanitizer in `wgp_patches.py:389-445` mmgp-specific, or does ComfyUI's LoRA loader trip on the same key shapes?",
    "Q7: Should we keep RIFE temporal interpolation vendored inside reigh-worker or wrap it as a VibeComfy custom node? Same for Uni3C ControlNet cache.",
    "Q8: Hunyuan ready_template authorship: who builds it, against which reference workflow, and on what timeline?",
    "Q9: Should the existing `comfy` task type be retired, kept as a thin pass-through, or repurposed as the canonical raw-API escape hatch?",
    "Q10: Should the dev default profile (currently 3) be aligned with production (1) before cutover?",
    "Q11: Does `reigh-worker/headless_model_management.py` have any non-WGP callers that need migration, or can it be deleted outright with `headless_wgp` in Sprint 8?",
    "Q12: Are the duplicate `headless_wgp` console-script entries at `pyproject.toml:109` and `pyproject.toml:165` an intentional cross-section registration or a copy-paste bug?"
  ],

    "Queue contracts (Supabase task table schema, claim/status update flow, output upload paths) remain unchanged across the migration \u2014 only the in-process generation backend swaps.",

    "VibeComfy at `./vibecomfy` is the canonical home for new templates and patches.",

  "signals": {
    "iteration": 5,
    "idea": "Produce a migration plan document for moving reigh-worker's execution backend from Wan2GP to VibeComfy. Repos live side-by-side at ./reigh-worker (current Wan2GP consumer) and ./vibecomfy (target). Plan must: (1) Audit current reigh-worker->Wan2GP integration: every task type, memory-profile system (lowvram/medvram/highvram/profiled/etc.), model load/unload lifecycle, queue/dispatch shape, error paths, telemetry, worker-orchestrator coupling. (2) Audit VibeComfy capabilities: ready_templates, VibeWorkflow IR, validation, embedded local execution, RunPod execution path, gaps vs Wan2GP (especially memory profiles). (3) Identify feature-parity gaps to close in VibeComfy before cutover (memory-profile abstraction modeled on Wan2GP's, equivalents for every task type, model-management lifecycle). (4) Sprint-by-sprint migration plan, each sprint <=2 weeks, sequenced for incremental risk retirement (shadow/dual-run/canary before full cutover), with concrete shippable outcomes. Cover: VibeComfy parity work, adapter/shim in reigh-worker, dual-execution+comparison harness, per-task-type cutover order with rationale, rollback plan, telemetry/observability changes, final Wan2GP removal. (5) Open questions, assumptions, decision points, risks, mitigations. Context: reigh-worker uses Wan2GP today as in-process execution backend across multiple task types (image gen, video gen, edits, etc.). Memory profiles are non-negotiable \u2014 full parity required before cutover. VibeComfy is a Python toolkit around ComfyUI workflows with VibeWorkflow IR, ready_templates, embedded-local + RunPod execution. Migration should preserve reigh-worker's external behavior (queue contracts, output shapes, latency SLOs). Orchestrator (./reigh-worker-orchestrator) provisions GPU workers; coordinate worker-image/runtime changes with that. Output: docs/migration-vibecomfy.md.",

        Known accepted debt grouped by subsystem:
        {
  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

    {

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],
